<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对阿拉伯诗歌的研究主要集中于分析任务（如韵律、标题预测），缺乏对实际创作层面（可控生成）的探索，而诗歌在阿拉伯文化中具有核心地位，因此需要开发辅助用户创作诗歌的实用能力。

Method: 1) 构建大规模指令数据集，覆盖现代标准阿拉伯语及多种方言，包含写作、修改、续写、分析等多任务；2) 基于指令微调LLM；3) 使用自动评估和母语者人工评估验证生成质量。

Result: 微调后的模型能够根据用户需求（如风格、韵律）生成对齐的诗歌，自动评估和母语者人工评估均表现良好。

Conclusion: 基于指令的数据集和微调方法能有效赋予LLM可控的阿拉伯诗歌生成能力，填补了该领域从分析到实用创作的空白，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Poetry has long been a central art form for Arabic speakers, serving as a powerful medium of expression and cultural identity. While modern Arabic speakers continue to value poetry, existing research on Arabic poetry within Large Language Models (LLMs) has primarily focused on analysis tasks such as interpretation or metadata prediction, e.g., rhyme schemes and titles. In contrast, our work addresses the practical aspect of poetry creation in Arabic by introducing controllable generation capabilities to assist users in writing poetry. Specifically, we present a large-scale, carefully curated instruction-based dataset in Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) and various Arabic dialects. This dataset enables tasks such as writing, revising, and continuing poems based on predefined criteria, including style and rhyme, as well as performing poetry analysis. Our experiments show that fine-tuning LLMs on this dataset yields models that can effectively generate poetry that is aligned with user requirements, based on both automated metrics and human evaluation with native Arabic speakers. The data and the code are available at https://github.com/mbzuai-nlp/instructpoet-ar

</details>


### [37] [Multi-Level Narrative Evaluation Outperforms Lexical Features for Mental Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27846)
*Yuxi Ma,Jieming Cui,Muyang Li,Ye Zhao,Yu Li,Yixuan Wang,Chi Zhang,Yinyin Zang,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出三层次分析框架（微观词汇特征、中观语义嵌入、宏观LLM叙事评估），并在830篇中文治疗性文本中证明宏观层评估在心理健康预测上显著优于词汇和嵌入特征，挑战了传统词计数方法的主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算叙事分析方法碎片化：词典工具忽略话语结构，嵌入技术混淆局部连贯与全局组织，缺乏将技术映射到叙事构建层级过程的统一框架。

Method: 构建三层次框架：微观层（词汇特征）、中观层（语义嵌入）、宏观层（LLM叙事评估），分析830篇涵盖抑郁、焦虑、创伤的中文治疗性文本。

Result: 宏观层叙事评估显著优于词汇和嵌入特征；形式结构特征（Labov故事语法、RST连贯性、命题构成）显示叙事组织本身携带预测信号；语义嵌入独立增量价值有限但在多层分类中有提升。

Conclusion: 将计算层级扎根于话语加工理论，识别出宏观结构组织是临床信号的主要载体，并为干预设计和纵向研究生成可检验假设。

Abstract: How people narrate their experiences offers a window into how the mind organizes them. Computational approaches to therapeutic writing have evolved from lexical counting to neural methods, yet remain fragmented: dictionary tools miss discourse structure, while embeddings conflate local coherence with global organization. No existing framework maps these techniques onto the hierarchical processes through which narratives are constructed. Here we introduce a three-level framework - micro-level lexical features, meso-level semantic embeddings, and macro-level LLM narrative evaluation - and show, across 830 Chinese therapeutic texts spanning depression, anxiety, and trauma, that macro-level evaluation substantially outperforms lexical and embedding features for mental health prediction. This challenges the field's emphasis on word-counting: formal structural features (Labov's story grammar, RST coherence, propositional composition) demonstrate that narrative organization per se carries predictive signal, while clinically-grounded narrative dimensions capture how psychological states are expressed through discourse. Semantic embeddings add minimal independent value but yield incremental gains in multi-level classification. By grounding computational levels in discourse processing theory, this framework identifies macro-structural organization as the primary locus of clinical signal and generates testable hypotheses for intervention design and longitudinal research.

</details>


### [38] [Reasoning over Object Descriptions Improves Coreference Resolution in Task-Based Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27850)
*Oier Ijurco,Oier Lopez de Lacalle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型的测试时推理方法，通过结构化元数据和对话历史进行逐步推理，在SIMMC 2.1数据集上提升了任务型对话系统的指代消解性能，在小样本和跨域场景下优于有监督编码器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务型对话中的指代消解面临跨领域泛化能力差、有监督模型过度拟合数据集特定伪影等问题，尤其在视觉接地环境中，复杂场景和多样化对象元数据使指代消解更具挑战性。

Method: 提出一种单模态测试时推理方法，利用大语言模型对详细对象元数据和对话历史进行逐步推理，以改进指代消解。在SIMMC 2.1数据集上进行实验，采用小样本设置和精心设计的提示工程。

Result: 大语言模型能够生成逐步推理过程，有效对齐对话上下文与场景中的对象；小样本下的测试时推理能泛化到未见场景和新对象；在跨域评估中优于基于编码器的有监督方法。

Conclusion: 结构化元数据和精心设计的提示工程对于增强任务型对话系统的鲁棒性和泛化能力至关重要。

Abstract: Task-based dialogue systems assist users in achieving specific goals, such as executing actions or retrieving information, through natural language interactions. Accurate coreference resolution is essential, as it involves identifying object references within the dialogue - a task that becomes increasingly challenging in visually grounded environments characterized by complex scenes and diverse object metadata. However, coreference resolution in task-based dialogue remains limited by poor generalization across domains and heavy reliance on supervised models that often overfit to dataset-specific artifacts. In this work, we propose a unimodal test-time reasoning approach that enables large language models (LLMs) to reason over detailed object metadata and dialogue history to improve coreference resolution. Empirical results on the SIMMC 2.1 dataset demonstrate that LLMs can generate step-by-step reasoning processes that effectively align dialogue context with objects present in the scene. Extensive experiments highlight the models' ability to link conversations and objects accurately. Moreover, we show that test-time reasoning under few-shot settings generalizes effectively to unseen scenarios and novel objects, outperforming encoder-based supervised methods in cross-domain evaluations. These findings underscore the critical role of structured metadata and careful prompt engineering in enhancing the robustness and generalization of task-oriented dialogue systems.

</details>


### [39] [Geometry-Calibrated Conformal Abstention for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27914)
*Rui Xu,Yi Chen,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Conformal Abstention (CA)的后处理框架，该框架基于保形预测思想，通过模型内部的表征几何来校准预测置信度，从而决定是否应拒绝回答查询，在有限样本下同时保证参与概率和回答正确性，实验显示条件正确率达75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在面对缺乏相关知识的问题时，往往会产生看似合理但实际错误的幻觉性回答，而非承认自己不知道答案。现有通过重新训练来奖励模型承认无知的方法会导致过于保守的行为，且由于评估基准稀缺，泛化性能差。

Method: 提出Conformal Abstention (CA)框架，将保形预测(CP)思想适配到开放生成任务中。与CP使用非一致性分数不同，CA基于预测置信度做出拒绝决策。为了更好对齐预测置信度与模型的无知程度，引入了基于模型内部表征几何的校准策略，通过度量知识在塑造回答过程中的参与程度来实现校准。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在选择性回答任务上实现了显著提升，条件正确率达到75%。

Conclusion: CA框架能够在不重新训练模型的情况下，有效识别模型缺乏相关知识的情况并选择拒绝回答，同时提供有限样本下的理论保证，是一种实用且高效的幻觉缓解方案。

Abstract: When language models lack relevant knowledge for a given query, they frequently generate plausible responses that can be hallucinations, rather than admitting being agnostic about the answer. Retraining models to reward admitting ignorance can lead to overly conservative behaviors and poor generalization due to scarce evaluation benchmarks. We propose a post hoc framework, Conformal Abstention (CA), adapted from conformal prediction (CP) to determine whether to abstain from answering a query. CA provides finite-sample guarantees on both the probability of participation (i.e., not abstaining) and the probability that the generated response is correct. Importantly, the abstention decision relies on prediction confidence rather than the non-conformity scores used in CP, which are intractable for open-ended generation. To better align prediction confidence with the model's ignorance, we introduce a calibration strategy using representation geometry within the model to measure knowledge involvement in shaping the response. Experiments demonstrate that we improve selective answering significantly with 75 percent conditional correctness.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Semantics: Measuring Fine-Grained Emotion Preservation in Small Language Model-Based Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27920)
*Dawid Wisniewski,Igor Czudy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种小语言模型（EuroLLM、Aya Expanse、Gemma）在回译过程中保留细粒度情感的能力，使用GoEmotions数据集（28个情感类别）对五种欧洲语言进行测试，并探究了情感感知提示的改进效果以及ModernBERT作为情感分类器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译中情感细微差别的保留仍是一个挑战，现有方法往往优先保证语义等价而牺牲情感 fidelity，需要系统评估小语言模型在跨语言情感保留方面的能力。

Method: 使用GoEmotions数据集（28类Reddit评论情感），在五种欧洲语言（德、法、西、意、波兰语）上进行回译实验；评估三种SLM（EuroLLM、Aya Expanse、Gemma）的情感保留能力；测试情感感知提示策略的效果；比较ModernBERT与传统BERT在情感分类评估中的表现。

Result: 三种SLM在回译中保留细粒度情感的能力评估结果，情感感知提示策略对情感保留效果的提升，以及ModernBERT相比传统BERT在情感分类评估中的性能表现。

Conclusion: 小语言模型在跨语言情感保留方面存在差异，情感感知提示可改善保留效果，ModernBERT是情感分类评估的有效工具。

Abstract: Preserving affective nuance remains a challenge in Machine Translation (MT), where semantic equivalence often takes precedence over emotional fidelity. This paper evaluates the performance of three state-of-the-art Small Language Models (SLMs) -- EuroLLM, Aya Expanse, and Gemma -- in maintaining fine-grained emotions during backtranslation. Using the GoEmotions dataset, which comprises Reddit comments across 28 distinct categories, we assess emotional preservation across five European languages: German, French, Spanish, Italian, and Polish. Specifically, we investigate (i) the inherent capability of these SLMs to retain emotional sentiment, (ii) the efficacy of emotion-aware prompting in improving preservation, and (iii) the performance of ModernBERT as a contemporary alternative to BERT for emotion classification in MT evaluation.

</details>


### [41] [Can AI Be a Good Peer Reviewer? A Survey of Peer Review Process, Evaluation, and the Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27924)
*Sihong Wu,Owen Jiang,Yilun Zhao,Tiansheng Hu,Yiling Ma,Kaiyan Zhang,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了利用大语言模型（LLM）辅助或自动化同行评审全流程（包括审稿生成、 rebuttal、元评审、修订及评估）的技术方法、数据集、建模选择及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审是多阶段流程，现有LLM进展促使研究者探索辅助或自动化各阶段的方法，但缺乏系统性综述来指导实践。

Method: 采用综述方法，对审稿生成（微调、基于智能体、强化学习等）、评审后任务（rebuttal、元评审、修订）以及评估方法（人工、基于参考、LLM评估、面向方面）进行归纳与对比分析。

Result: 系统梳理了各类方法的技术路线、数据集和建模选择，指出了现有方法的局限性、伦理问题及未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为构建、评估和集成LLM系统以支持完整同行评审工作流提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Peer review is a multi-stage process involving reviews, rebuttals, meta-reviews, final decisions, and subsequent manuscript revisions. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have motivated methods that assist or automate different stages of this pipeline. In this survey, we synthesize techniques for (i) peer review generation, including fine-tuning strategies, agent-based systems, RL-based methods, and emerging paradigms to enhance generation; (ii) after-review tasks including rebuttals, meta-review and revision aligned to reviews; and (iii) evaluation methods spanning human-centered, reference-based, LLM-based and aspect-oriented. We catalog datasets, compare modeling choices, and discuss limitations, ethical concerns, and future directions. The survey aims to provide practical guidance for building, evaluating, and integrating LLM systems across the full peer review workflow.

</details>


### [42] [DPN-LE: Dual Personality Neuron Localization and Editing for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27929)
*Lifan Zheng,Xue Yang,Jiawei Chen,Chenyan Wu,Jingyuan Zhang,Fanheng Kong,Xinyi Zeng,Xiang Chen,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DPN-LE方法，通过对比高低特质样本的MLP激活值，定位并编辑与人格特质相关的稀疏神经元子集（约0.5%），在LLaMA-3-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上实现精准人格控制且保留通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经元编辑的人格编辑方法需修改大量神经元，导致模型性能显著下降。本文探究是否所有被修改的神经元都直接与人格表征相关，并试图找到更精确的人格相关神经元的定位与编辑方法。

Method: 通过对比高特质和低特质样本的MLP激活值，计算Cohen's d效应量和激活幅度构建逐层引导向量，采用双重标准筛选出互斥的神经元子集，对稀疏神经元进行线性干预实现推理时人格控制。

Result: 仅使用每特质1000个对比样本对，干预约0.5%的神经元，在保持竞争性人格控制的同时，显著提升了推理任务上的能力保留。

Conclusion: 验证了神经元的多功能性，发现对立人格特质呈现互斥的表征模式，提出的DPN-LE方法在人格编辑任务中实现精准控制与能力保留的最佳平衡。

Abstract: With the widespread adoption of large language models (LLMs), understanding their personality representation mechanisms has become critical. As a novel paradigm in Personality Editing, most existing methods employ neuron-editing to locate and modify LLM neurons, requiring changes to numerous neurons and leading to significant performance degradation. This raises a fundamental question: Are all modified neurons directly related to personality representation? In this work, we investigate and quantify this specificity through assessments of general capability impact and representation-level patterns. We find that: 1) Current methods can change personalities but reduce overall performance. 2) Neurons are multifunctional, connecting personality traits and general knowledge. 3) Opposing personality traits demonstrate distinctly mutually exclusive representation patterns. Motivated by these findings, we propose DPN-LE (Dual Personality Neuron Localization and Editing), which identifies personality-specific neurons by contrasting MLP activations between high-trait and low-trait samples. DPN-LE constructs layer-wise steering vectors and applies dual-criterion filtering based on Cohen's $d$ effect size and activation magnitude to isolate mutually exclusive neuron subsets. Sparse linear intervention on these neurons enables precise personality control at inference time. Using only 1,000 contrastive sample pairs per trait, DPN-LE intervenes on $\sim$0.5\% of neurons while achieving competitive personality control and substantially better capability preservation across reasoning tasks. Experiments on LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of our approach.

</details>


### [43] [Reliable Answers for Recurring Questions: Boosting Text-to-SQL Accuracy with Template Constrained Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28028)
*Smit Jivani,Sarvam Maheshwari,Sunita Sarawagi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TeCoD系统通过将历史NL-SQL对转化为可复用模板，并结合基于自然语言推理的模板选择模块与分区约束解码策略，在Text-to-SQL任务中相比ICL实现了最高36%的执行准确率提升和2.2倍延迟降低。

Conclusion: TeCoD通过模板约束解码有效解决了LLM在Text-to-SQL任务中的准确性和有效性挑战，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized Text-to-SQL generation, allowing users to query structured data using natural language with growing ease. Yet, real-world deployment remains challenging, especially in complex or unseen schemas, due to inconsistent accuracy and the risk of generating invalid SQL. We introduce Template Constrained Decoding (TeCoD), a system that addresses these limitations by harnessing the recurrence of query patterns in labeled workloads. TeCoD converts historical NL-SQL pairs into reusable templates and introduces a robust template selection module that uses a fine-tuned natural language inference model to match or reject queries efficiently. Once the template is selected, TeCoD enforces it during SQL generation through grammar-constrained decoding, implemented via a novel partitioned strategy that ensures both syntactic validity and efficiency. Together, these components yield up to 36% higher execution accuracy than in-context learning (ICL) and 2.2x lower latency on matched queries.

</details>


### [44] [Models Recall What They Violate: Constraint Adherence in Multi-Turn LLM Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28031)
*Garvin Kruthof*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DriftBench是一个用于评估多轮LLM辅助科学构思中约束遵循情况的基准测试。通过对来自5个提供商的7个模型、4种交互条件和24个科学领域的38份研究简报进行2,146次评分运行测试，发现迭代压力会可靠地增加结构复杂性并降低对原始约束的遵循。模型存在"知晓但违反"现象，即能准确复述约束却同时违反它们，KBV率从8%到99%不等。结构化检查点可以部分降低KBV率但无法消除分离，且复杂性膨胀仍然存在。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员与大型语言模型迭代优化想法时，模型能否保持对原始目标的保真度是一个关键问题。现有研究缺乏系统评估LLM在多轮科学构思过程中约束遵循能力的基准测试。

Method: 构建DriftBench基准测试，包含38份来自24个科学领域的研究简报，对7个模型（含2个开源权重）在4种交互条件下进行2,146次评分运行。设计了"知晓但违反率"（KBV rate）来测量约束非遵循与保留召回之间的分离，并采用人类验证确认LLM评判者的检测偏差。

Result: 迭代压力可靠地增加结构复杂性并降低对原始约束的遵循；模型存在"知晓但违反"现象，KBV率在8%-99%之间；结构化检查点部分降低KBV率但无法消除分离；复杂性膨胀持续存在；人类验证确认LLM评判者低估了约束违反情况。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在多轮科学构思中存在约束遵循与陈述性召回之间的分离现象，需要更好的机制来确保模型在迭代过程中保持对原始目标的保真度。

Abstract: When researchers iteratively refine ideas with large language models, do the models preserve fidelity to the original objective? We introduce DriftBench, a benchmark for evaluating constraint adherence in multi-turn LLM-assisted scientific ideation. Across 2,146 scored benchmark runs spanning seven models from five providers (including two open-weight), four interaction conditions, and 38 research briefs from 24 scientific domains, we find that iterative pressure reliably increases structural complexity and often reduces adherence to original constraints. A restatement probe reveals a dissociation between declarative recall and behavioral adherence, as models accurately restate constraints they simultaneously violate. The knows-but-violates (KBV) rate, measuring constraint non-compliance despite preserved recall, ranges from 8% to 99% across models. Structured checkpointing partially reduces KBV rates but does not close the dissociation, and complexity inflation persists. Human validation against blind raters confirms that the LLM judge under-detects constraint violations, making reported constraint adherence scores conservative. Sensitivity analyses confirm the findings are robust to temperature (0.7 vs.\ 1.0) and pressure type (novelty vs.\ rigor). We release all briefs, prompts, rubrics, transcripts, and scores as an open benchmark.

</details>


### [45] [Ease of dependency distance minimization in star-like structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28034)
*Emília Garcia-Casademont,Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了句法依存树中星型结构的距离最小化优化问题，发现星型树的优化景观实际上是凸函数（比之前认为的拟凸更简单），并论证了反距离最小化效应源于竞争原则而非优化困难，且星型结构上距离最小化的收益较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解答两个核心问题：(1)最小化依存距离的难度有多大？(2)为什么星型结构中存在反依存距离最小化效应，而路径结构中没有？

Method: 通过理论分析证明星型树和准星型树的优化景观是凸函数（而非先前认为的拟凸），并从竞争原则和收益角度解释反最小化效应。

Result: (1)星型树的优化景观是凸函数，优化问题比先前认为的更简单；(2)反距离最小化效应的真正原因是竞争原则，而非优化难度；星型结构上距离最小化的收益较低。

Conclusion: 星型结构的依存距离最小化优化比预期更简单（凸函数），其反最小化效应应归因于竞争原则和低收益，而非优化难度。

Abstract: The syntactic structure of a sentence can be represented as a tree where edges indicate syntactic dependencies between words. When that structure is a star, it has been demonstrated that the head should be placed in the middle of the linear arrangement according to the principle of syntactic dependency distance minimization. However, hubs of stars tend to be put at one of the ends, against that principle. Here we address two questions: (1) How difficult is it to minimize dependency distance? (2) Why anti dependency distance minimization effects have been found in star structures but not in path structures? The ease of optimization is determined by the shape of the optimization landscape. It was demonstrated that the landscape of star structures is quasiconvex (Ferrer-i-Cancho 2015, Language Dynamics and Change). As for (1), here we show that it is indeed convex (a particular case of quasiconvexity) both for star trees and quasistar trees and thus the distance-based optimization problem is simpler than previously believed. As for (2), we argue that (a) competing principles, rather than the difficulty of optimization, must be the actual reason for anti-dependency distance minimization effects and that (b) dependency distance minimization on star-like structures is less rewarding compared to other structures.

</details>


### [46] [Repetition over Diversity: High-Signal Data Filtering for Sample-Efficient German Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28075)
*Ansar Aynetdinov,Patrick Haller,Alan Akbik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对德语等非英语高资源语言，研究发现多epoch重复高质量过滤数据始终优于单次遍历大规模低质量数据，即使重复7个epoch后性能差距依然存在。基于此方法训练的Boldt模型在训练量仅为同类模型1/10至1/360的情况下达到了SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非英语高资源语言（以德语为例）在数据过滤时面临的质量-多样性权衡：是采用大量轻过滤数据的单次训练以最大化多样性，还是严格过滤出高质量核心数据并多epoch重复训练以追求质量。

Method: 对5亿德语网页文档构建层次化质量过滤器，比较多epoch训练过滤子集与单次遍历大规模语料的效果；在多种模型规模和token预算下进行实验验证。

Result: 重复高质量数据始终优于单次遍历更大但过滤较少的语料集；即使重复7个epoch，性能优势依然保持；Boldt模型在训练token数仅为同类模型1/10-1/360的情况下取得SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 对非英语大语言模型而言，通过质量过滤实现语义集中的方法比单纯最大化独特数据量更有效，为高效语言建模提供了更可行的路径。

Abstract: Recent research has shown that filtering massive English web corpora into high-quality subsets significantly improves training efficiency. However, for high-resource non-English languages like German, French, or Japanese, aggressive filtering creates a strategic dilemma: should practitioners prioritize diversity by training once on large amounts of lightly filtered web data, or prioritize quality by strictly filtering for a high-quality core and repeating it over multiple epochs? We investigate this trade-off for German by constructing hierarchical quality filters applied to 500M web documents, comparing multi-epoch training on the filtered subsets against single-pass training on a diverse corpus. Our experiments across multiple model scales and token budgets show that repeating high-quality data consistently outperforms single-pass training on larger, less filtered sets. Notably, the performance gap persists even after 7 epochs. Our findings suggest that for non-English LLMs, semantic concentration through quality filtering offers a more viable path to efficient language modeling than simply maximizing unique data volume. We release our German language models (called Boldt), as well as our cleaned evaluation benchmarks to the research community. Our experiments indicate that they achieve state-of-the-art results despite training on 10-360x fewer tokens than comparable models.

</details>


### [47] [TopBench: A Benchmark for Implicit Prediction and Reasoning over Tabular Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28076)
*An-Yang Ji,Jun-Peng Jiang,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TopBench基准，用于评估LLM在隐式预测性表格问答任务中的表现。实验发现当前模型在意图识别上存在困难，常退化为简单查找，而准确的意图消歧是预测行为的前提，提升预测精度需要更复杂的建模或推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的表格问答查询常具有隐式预测性质（需从历史模式推断未观测答案），而非简单的信息检索或聚合。现有基准缺乏对此类任务的评估，因此需要构建相应的基准来测试LLM的能力。

Method: 构建了包含779个样本、四个子任务（单点预测、决策制定、处理效应分析、复杂过滤）的TopBench基准。评估了多种LLM在基于文本和智能体工作流两种模式下的表现，并进行深入的错误分析。

Result: 当前模型在隐式预测任务中常默认执行查找操作而非预测，准确进行意图消歧是实现预测行为的前提，而要提升预测精度的上限需要更复杂的建模或推理能力。

Conclusion: LLM在隐式预测表格问答中仍存在显著不足，主要瓶颈在于意图识别而非执行能力，未来需要更强大的推理或建模机制来突破预测精度的上限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced Table Question Answering, where most queries can be answered by extracting information or simple aggregation. However, a common class of real-world queries is implicitly predictive, requiring the inference of unobserved answers from historical patterns rather than mere retrieval. These queries introduce two challenges: recognizing latent intent and reliable predictive reasoning over massive tables. To assess LLMs in such Tabular questiOn answering with implicit Prediction tasks, we introduce TopBench, a benchmark consisting of 779 samples across four sub-tasks, ranging from single-point prediction to decision making, treatment effect analysis, and complex filtering, requiring models to generate outputs spanning reasoning text and structured tables. We evaluate diverse models under both text-based and agentic workflows. Experiments reveal that current models often struggle with intent recognition, defaulting to just lookups. Deeper analysis identifies that accurate intent disambiguation serves as the prerequisite for leading these predictive behaviors. Furthermore, elevating the upper bound of prediction precision requires the integration of more sophisticated modeling or reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [48] [On the Proper Treatment of Units in Surprisal Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28147)
*Samuel Kiegeland,Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Tim Vieira,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出surprisal理论中“单位”概念定义模糊，实验中选择的语言学单位（如词）与语言模型的token化方式不一致，导致surprisal预测隐式混合了分析单位定义和兴趣区域选择两个建模决策。作者提出一个统一框架，将tokenization视为实现细节而非科学原语，呼吁明确分离这些选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Surprisal理论中“单位”概念长期被模糊处理，实验研究中语言学单位与预训练语言模型的token化方式不匹配，导致方法学上的混淆与不一致。

Method: 提出一个统一的理论框架，将分析单位定义与兴趣区域选择这两个原本混淆的建模决策明确分离，把tokenization降至实现细节层面。

Result: 给出了在任意单位清单上推理surprisal的通用框架，并指出当前实践中隐式采用的ad hoc程序的问题根源。

Conclusion: Surprisal分析应明确区分单位定义和兴趣区域选择，tokenization不应被视为科学原语，而只是实现细节。

Abstract: Surprisal theory links human processing effort to the predictability of an upcoming linguistic unit, but empirical work often leaves the notion of a unit underspecified. In practice, experimental stimuli are segmented into linguistically motivated units (e.g., words), while pretrained language models assign probability mass to a fixed token alphabet that typically does not align with those units. As a result, surprisal-based predictors depend implicitly on ad hoc procedures that conflate two distinct modeling choices: the definition of the unit of analysis and the choice of regions of interest over which predictions are evaluated. In this paper, we disentangle these choices and give a unified framework for reasoning about surprisal over arbitrary unit inventories. We argue that surprisal-based analyses should make these choices explicit and treat tokenization as an implementation detail rather than a scientific primitive.

</details>

### [49] [Man, Machine, and Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27052)
*Akshunna S. Dogra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文尝试构建一个统一的学习理论框架，将学习解释为多层级互联过程（问题设定、方法选择、优化动力学分析），提出"可解问题"的精确定义和"参数化方法"概念，并勾勒"通用收敛定理"以说明可解问题何时及如何能被所选方法求解，最终将学习研究简化为动力学、几何和基础物理中已有的少量思想与工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受深度学习在众多领域成功以及物理学用少量基础理论解释大量现象的启发，作者希望建立一个稀疏统一的框架来指导学习、优化和建模领域的讨论与发现。

Method: 将学习视为多层级互联过程：问题设定（提出"可解问题"的精确定义）、方法选择（定义"参数化方法"）、以及通过优化动力学分析二者的相互作用。在此基础上尝试勾勒"通用收敛定理"。

Result: 发现这些构造将学习研究简化为少数思想与工具，其中许多可直接从动力系统理论、几何学和基础物理中适配而来。

Conclusion: 存在一种可能性，即可以用一个稀疏的统一框架来统摄学习、优化和建模领域，其核心思想源自动力学系统理论、几何学和基础物理。

Abstract: Nonlinear models and optimization methods have successfully tackled a rapidly growing set of problems in recent years. Indeed, a relatively small toolbox of such models and methods can provide sufficient performance across a large landscape of tasks: deep learning alone has made significant recent contributions in scientific modelling, natural language processing, visual analysis, etc. A similar relationship exists between physical theories and phenomena, where many applications and observations emerge neatly from remarkably minimal foundations. It is natural to wonder if sparse unified frameworks could be built to steer discussion and discovery in the fields concerned with learning, optimization, and modelling. In this work, we posit and examine a possible outline for such a unified theory, interpreting the notion of ''learning'' in a broad sense. In particular, we pursue our goals by viewing learning as an inter-connected process on multiple levels: problem setup, choosing methods, and the analysis of their interplay via imposed optimisation dynamics. We begin by proposing a precise yet versatile definition for ''solvable'' problems. We then define the ''parametrised methods'' by which their solution(s) may be ''learned''. Our goal is to sketch a ''universal convergence theorem'', specifying how and when solvable problems become amenable to the methods chosen for them. We find these constructions reduce the study of learning down to remarkably few ideas and tools - many of which are simply adapted from existing ones in dynamical systems theory, geometry, and fundamental physics.

</details>


### [50] [Nonsmooth Riemannian optimization with inexact manifold primitives via bundle methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27078)
*Mateo Díaz,Benjamin Grimmer,Ian McPherson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Hadamard流形上的非光滑测地凸优化问题，提出了使用近似指数映射和平行传输的近端束方法，并首次建立了仅依赖次梯度和近似原始算子的Oracle复杂度界，获得了次线性收敛率和在尖锐函数增长下的最优线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Hadamard流形上的优化方法要么需要精确的指数映射和并行传输（计算昂贵），要么缺乏非渐近收敛率保证。为了降低计算成本并保证理论性能，需要一种能容忍近似原始算子且具有非渐近复杂度界的优化方法。

Method: 提出一种近端束方法（proximal bundle method），该方法仅使用次梯度和非精确的原始算子（一阶近似指数映射和向量传输），在Hadamard流形上进行非光滑测地凸优化。

Result: 对于一般目标函数获得了次线性收敛率；在尖锐函数增长（sharp function growth）条件下获得了最优的线性收敛率。这是首次仅依赖次梯度和非精确原始算子得到Oracle复杂度界。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了Hadamard流形优化中理论保证与实用近似之间的鸿沟，证明了使用一阶近似原始算子仍能保持非渐近收敛保证，使得流形优化在计算上更加可行。

Abstract: Optimization on Hadamard manifolds -- the natural Riemannian setting for globally geodesically convex problems -- relies on exponential maps to retract tangent vectors and parallel transport to connect tangent spaces across the manifold. These primitives are often computationally expensive, leading software packages to rely on approximations: first-order retractions and vector transports. However, existing results for optimization on Hadamard manifolds either require exact primitives or lack non-asymptotic rates. We bridge this gap by introducing a proximal bundle method for nonsmooth geodesically convex optimization and establishing the first oracle-complexity bounds that rely only on subgradients and inexact primitives. We obtain sublinear rates for general objectives and optimal linear convergence under sharp function growth.

</details>


### [51] [A Scale-Shape Dual Newton Method for Entropic Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27154)
*Nicholas Barnfield,James V. Burke,Michael P. Friedlander,Tim Hoheisel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种带阻尼的精确牛顿法，用于求解非负象限上的熵正则化最小二乘问题，该方法具有全局线性收敛（O(log ε⁻¹)迭代复杂度）、局部超线性到二次收敛速率，且能避免有限精度溢出问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典对偶求解器受限于有限精度溢出，需要一种免疫该问题的全局收敛算法。

Method: 对原始变量进行尺度-方向分解，得到具有非奇异雅可比的对偶问题；利用稳定的log-sum-exp和softmax原语评估目标函数和雅可比矩阵；通过Lambert W界均匀控制雅可比谱。

Result: 全局线性收敛（O(log ε⁻¹)迭代复杂度）、局部超线性到二次收敛；解映射关于数据、正则化参数和参考测度联合Lipschitz连续，且连续延拓到零正则化极限。

Conclusion: 实验验证了该方法的溢出鲁棒性和收敛行为，适用于量子蒙特卡罗数据解析延拓问题。

Abstract: We give a damped inexact Newton method for entropy-regularized least-squares on the nonnegative orthant that converges globally at a linear rate with $O(\logε^{-1})$ iteration complexity, locally at a superlinear-to-quadratic rate, and is immune to the finite-precision overflow that limits classical dual solvers. A scale-shape decomposition of the primal -- separating its scale from its direction -- produces a dual with a nonsingular Jacobian. Objectives and Jacobians are evaluated through stable log-sum-exp and softmax primitives. Lambert W bounds on the scale uniformly control the Jacobian's spectrum, from which both rates follow. The solution map is jointly Lipschitz in the data, regularization parameter, and reference measure, and extends continuously to the vanishing-regularization limit. Experiments on a problem from analytic continuation of quantum Monte Carlo data confirm the predicted overflow resilience and convergence behavior.

</details>


### [52] [Boundedness of solutions in feedback systems with antithetic controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27290)
*Moh Kamalul Wafi,Arthur C. B. de Oliveira,Eduardo D. Sontag*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类含延迟反馈的非线性系统（源于合成生物学中的拮抗反馈控制器）的所有轨迹均有界，核心机制是当被控状态过大且持续过久时，延迟反馈环路会最终响应并将其压制回有界区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成生物学中拮抗反馈控制器通过延迟反馈环路调控生物过程，需要保证系统状态不会无界增长，从而确保生物系统的安全性和可预测性。

Abstract: This paper studies whether solutions of a class of nonlinear feedback systems remain bounded over time. Our main result is that every trajectory of such a system is bounded. The key insight is simple: if the regulated state grows too large for too long, the feedback loop will eventually respond and push it back down. More precisely, we show that whenever the state exceeds a threshold and remains there long enough, the feedback signal becomes strong enough to force the state to decrease. We then show that once this happens, the feedback remains strong enough to keep the state from growing unbounded. The proof works directly with differential inequalities and does not require constructing a Lyapunov function, making the mechanism transparent and easy to interpret. The boundedness result can be understood as a time-domain small-gain effect, where the delayed feedback ultimately counteracts any persistent growth in the system.

Abstract: We study the set of solutions to a parameterized, strongly convex optimization problem whose cost depends on uncertain, bounded parameters. Abstract: This paper investigates the continuous-time counterpart of the Q-function for entropy-regularized mean-field control (MFC) with controlled common noise, coined as q-function by Jia and Zhou (2023) in the single agent's model. We first show that, under discretely sampled actions, the value function in the exploratory formulation converges to the one in the relaxed control formulation as the time grid refines. Leveraging the relaxed control formulation, we derive the exploratory Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation, in which the controlled common noise gives rise to an additional nonlinear functional of policy, rendering the policy iteration intricate. Under certain concavity condition, we establish the existence and uniqueness of the optimal one-step policy iteration via a first-order condition using the partial linear functional derivative with respect to policy. The policy improvement at each iteration is verified by relating to an entropy-regularized optimization problem over the space of policies. In the mean-field setting, we introduce the integrated q-function (Iq-function) defined on the state distribution and the policy, and it is shown that an optimal policy is identified as a two-layer fixed point to the argmax operator of the Iq-function. Finally, we provide the explicit characterization of an optimal policy as a Gaussian distribution in the general linear-quadratic (LQ) setting.

Abstract: This paper is a continuation work of Ren et al. (2026) aiming to further devise q-learning algorithms for mean-field control (MFC) with controlled common noise. Based on the relaxed control formulation, we first establish the martingale condition of the value function and the Iq-function by evaluating along the conditional state distributions generated by all test policies. As the data in the relaxed control formulation are not observable in practice, we quantify the error incurred when they are replaced by the observable ones in the exploratory formulation under discretely sampled actions. This, together with a two-layer fixed point characterization of an optimal policy in Ren et al. (2026), allows us to propose several algorithms including the Actor-Critic q-learning algorithm, in which the policy is updated in the Actor-step based on the iteration rule induced by the improved Iq-function, and the value function and Iq-function are updated in the Critic-step based on the martingale orthogonality condition using the data from the exploratory formulation. We also establish the convergence of the inner iterations in the Actor-step in an infinite-horizon linear quadratic (LQ) framework. In two examples, within and beyond LQ framework, our q-learning algorithms are implemented with satisfactory performance.

Abstract: Across science and engineering, mean-field methods have been a powerful and versatile approach for the analysis of systems of many interacting elements. However, common arguments used to characterize an infinite population limit can be quite restrictive from a modeling perspective by requiring that all agents be identical (i.e. symmetric, or homogeneous). In this paper, we consider large interactive particle systems under agent heterogeneity for a class of discrete time teams composed of finitely many species of agents, grouped into symmetric subteams, called clusters. In particular, for the class of discounted, partially exchangeable cost criteria considered, we establish the optimality of centralized joint policies which are exchangeable within each cluster and depend on the agent ensemble only up to the state empirical distribution over each cluster. Following this, a generalization of De Finetti's theorem is used to demonstrate the subsequential convergence of these optimal policies to one which is decentralized (depending on only the local state and distribution over each cluster) and symmetric within each subteam as the population size approaches infinity. This solution is shown to induce a sequence of asymptotically optimal policies for the finite population problems which retain their structure and decentralization. Furthermore, our analysis justifies the optimality of a decentralized McKean-Vlasov team representation involving coupled representative agents for each of the clusters, and establishes a verification theorem/value iterations for the mean-field limit. In this way, we provide an avenue for analyzing complex, cooperative systems with finite heterogeneity and set the stage for further research on learning algorithms.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a regularized Hessian-free Newton-type method for minimizing smooth convex functions with Lipschitz continuous Hessians. The algorithm constructs an approximate Hessian by finite differences and selects the regularization parameter through an adaptive criterion that ensures sufficient decrease and gradient control. We prove that the method achieves a global $\mathcal{O}(k^{-2})$ convergence rate, matching the best known bound for second-order methods. A modified variant incorporating the exact Hessian when available enjoys local quadratic convergence under standard assumptions. Despite its simplicity, this variant is computationally faster than the \emph{Regularized Newton Method} of Mishchenko (2023) across several convex benchmark problems. Our analysis also provides explicit bounds on the regularization sequence and a worst-case iteration complexity of order $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$. The proposed framework thus unifies regularized and Hessian-free Newton-type schemes, offering a theoretically sound and practically efficient alternative for smooth convex optimization.

Abstract: Convex maximization encompasses a broad class of optimization problems and is generally NP-hard, even for low-rank objectives. This paper investigates structural conditions under which convex maximization becomes polynomially solvable. From a geometric perspective, we introduce comonotonicity, a structural property of the feasible region crucial for problem tractability, and establish mathematical characterizations of this property. Under comonotonicity and mild additional assumptions, we develop a unified enumerative framework showing that fixed-rank convex maximization is polynomially solvable. This viewpoint recovers several known tractability results that previously required separate analyses, such as fixed-rank convex matroid maximization and sparse principal component analysis (SPCA). Furthermore, for the more structured class of standard comonotone feasible regions, we refine the analysis via a lifting technique to achieve a square-root improvement in the complexity bound. Finally, applications to SPCA and its variants illustrate the broad applicability and effectiveness of the proposed framework.

Abstract: Modern stochastic optimization pipelines increasingly rely on learned generative models to represent uncertainty, while downstream decisions are evaluated almost entirely through Monte Carlo scenarios. This shifts the operational object of uncertainty from an explicit probability law to the sampler induced by the learned generator. Reliability therefore depends on two errors: sampler misspecification and finite-simulation error. We propose Sampler-Robust Optimization (SRO), which optimizes decisions against the worst-case sampler induced by perturbing the learned generator. This sampler-first formulation aligns with simulation-based decision pipelines and admits a sharpness-aware interpretation: it favors decisions whose performance is stable under generator perturbations, rather than merely under the nominal sampler. Under a coverage assumption, we show that the empirical worst-case objective provides a high-probability upper certificate for the true population objective, with finite-simulation error partially absorbed by the robustification used to guard against sampler misspecification. The framework accommodates generative models with or without explicit densities and admits efficient minimax procedures. Portfolio-optimization experiments show that SRO produces more stable decisions and improves out-of-sample performance under distribution shift.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微分博弈中，已知博弈策略反推成本函数（即逆问题）在经济学、工程学等领域具有重要应用，但针对描述子动态系统（奇异系统）的逆问题研究尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先建立无限时域线性二次型描述子动态微分博弈的数学模型，基于观测到的反馈策略轮廓，通过理论推导刻画所有能使该策略成为反馈纳什均衡的成本函数集合（解集），并证明其矩形性和凸性；随后设计算法从该解集中计算一个可行实现。

Result: 成功刻画了描述子动态系统下微分博弈逆问题的解集，证明了其矩形与凸性结构，并给出了可计算实现的具体算法。

Conclusion: 本文为描述子动态微分博弈的逆问题提供了完整的理论框架和计算方法，为后续相关应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this letter, we study a model-based inverse problem for infinite-horizon linear-quadratic differential games with descriptor dynamics. Specifically, we seek to identify the set of all cost functions that rationalize an observed feedback strategy profile of the players as a feedback Nash equilibrium, referred to here as the solution set. We characterize the solution set, show that it is rectangular and convex, and provide an algorithm to compute an admissible realization. Finally, we illustrate our results with numerical examples.

</details>

Method: 将等式约束优化的一阶KKT条件重构为控制仿射动力系统，优化变量作为状态，拉格朗日乘子作为控制输入，等式约束定义为滑模面。采用滑模控制设计保证约束在有限时间内精确满足；引入非奇异终端滑模范数梯度流加速收敛到最优解。通过Lyapunov方法进行稳定性与收敛性分析。

Abstract: This paper develops a sliding mode control based frame work for equality constrained optimization by reformulation the first order Karush Kuhn Tucker conditions as control affine dynamical system. The optimization variables are treated as states and the Lagrange multipliers as control input, with equality constraints defined as sliding manifold. The resulting design guarantees exact constraint enforcement with finite time convergence, independent of objective convexity, and exhibits robustness to matched disturbance, structural uncertainty and bounded measurement noise. To accelerate the convergence, a nonsingular terminal sliding mode based normed gradient flow is introduced, ensuring both finite time convergence to optimal solution and constraint satisfaction. Rigorous Lyapunov analysis establishes closed loop stability and convergence. Numerical studies across diverse benchmark problems demonstrate superior accuracy and robustness over classical continuous time optimization method, highlighting effectiveness under disturbance.

</details>


### [62] [Data-Driven Continuous-Time Linear Quadratic Regulator via Closed-Loop and Reinforcement Learning Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27922)
*Armin Gießler,Felix Thömmes,Sören Hohmann*

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间线性二次型调节器（LQR）问题的两种数据驱动参数化方法：闭环参数化（CL）和积分强化学习参数化（IRL），并提供了统一的处理框架，揭示了二者之间的结构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将离散时间系统中已广泛研究的闭环参数化方法扩展到连续时间设置，并统一理解两种主流数据驱动LQR方法之间的结构关系。

Method: 基于闭环参数化推导了策略迭代方案、数据驱动连续时间代数Riccati方程（CARE）及凸优化问题；基于IRL参数化推导了策略梯度流和凸重构；最后对两种参数化进行了统一处理。

Result: 将闭环参数化成功适配到连续时间系统，提出了策略迭代、数据驱动CARE和凸问题形式；在IRL框架下获得了策略梯度流和凸重构；建立了两种参数化的统一视角。

Conclusion: 两种参数化方法均可实现连续时间LQR问题的数据驱动求解，统一框架有助于系统理解现有方法及其结构关联。

Abstract: This paper studies data-driven approaches to the continuous-time linear quadratic regulator (LQR) problem based on two existing parameterizations, namely a closed-loop (CL) parameterization from behavioral system theory and an integral reinforcement learning (IRL) parameterization. The CL parameterization characterizes the closed-loop system via a matrix that satisfies equality constraints. While this parameterization has been extensively studied for discrete-time systems, we adapt key results to the continuous-time setting and develop a policy iteration (PI) scheme, derive a data-driven continuous-time algebraic Riccati equation (CARE), and introduce an alternative convex problem formulation. The IRL parameterization utilizes off-policy data to perform policy evaluation, which is then used for PI or value iteration. Within the IRL framework, we derive a policy gradient flow and propose convex reformulations of the LQR problem. Finally, we provide a unified treatment of these parameterizations that enables a systematic understanding of existing approaches and clarifies their structural relationships.

</details>


### [63] [Frank-Wolfe Beyond 1/t Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28006)
*Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出局部对偶尖锐性（LDS）条件，证明在满足该条件的可行集下，Frank-Wolfe算法对于任意光滑凸函数都能实现优于O(1/t)的收敛速度，打破了经典的Ω(1/t)下界，并通过对均匀凸集的实例验证，首次给出无条件o(1/t)收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典下界表明Frank-Wolfe算法在一般光滑凸情形下存在Ω(1/t)的收敛屏障，突破该屏障通常需要强凸性或Hölder误差界等有利函数性质。本文试图通过发掘可行区域的几何特性，在不依赖函数特殊性质的前提下实现加速收敛。

Method: 定义局部对偶尖锐性（LDS）条件——一种可行区域及其线性化黑箱（LMO）的几何性质，可视为集合均匀凸性的推广与局部化。在此条件下分析Frank-Wolfe算法的收敛行为，并与局部Hölder误差界等函数性质结合以获得量化速率。

Result: 在LDS条件下，Frank-Wolfe算法对任意光滑凸函数实现o(1/t)收敛，排除了Ω(1/t)下界的可能性；特别地，对均匀凸集首次给出无条件o(1/t)收敛结果。结合更强函数性质可进一步量化具体收敛速率。

Conclusion: LDS条件从可行区域的几何角度为Frank-Wolfe算法提供了突破经典下界的新途径，揭示了集合结构在算法加速中的关键作用，统一并推广了均匀凸性等已有概念。

Abstract: We consider smooth convex minimization over compact convex sets, i.e., $\min_{x \in C} f(x)$ with the (vanilla) Frank-Wolfe algorithm. Well-known lower bounds establish a worst-case $Ω(1/t)$ primal-gap barrier in the general smooth convex case, and faster convergence usually requires favorable function properties such as Hölder error bounds or strong convexity. We present a new Local Dual Sharpness (LDS) condition, essentially a property of the feasible region and its LMO, under which the Frank-Wolfe algorithm converges in $o(1/t)$ for any smooth convex function, ruling out an $Ω(1/t)$ lower bound under LDS. The condition is a generalization (and localization) of uniform convexity of sets and it is satisfied by any uniformly convex set. To our knowledge, this is the first unconditional $o(1/t)$ convergence result for uniformly convex sets. Combining LDS with stronger function properties, e.g., a local variant of Hölder error bounds, allows us to quantify the actual rates.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法在处理大规模约束极小化问题时，尤其是在目标函数为强拟凸或伪凸情形下，收敛速度或计算效率存在不足，因此需要设计一种新型高效算法。

Result: 在强拟凸目标函数下，算法生成的序列具有线性收敛率；在大规模分式规划和二次规划的数值实验中，算法性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的缩放梯度非单调线搜索方法在理论和数值上均表现良好，适用于强拟凸和伪凸约束极小化问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a scaled gradient modified non-monotone line search method for solving constrained minimization problems, and explore several specific properties of this method, namely, its convergence analysis. We discuss the linear convergence rate of the sequence generated by the proposed algorithm to a solution of the constrained minimization problem where the objective function is strongly quasiconvex. We consider numerical examples of large-scale fractional programming and quadratic programming for the function of pseudo convex and strongly quasiconvex and compare the performance of the proposed algorithm with the existing ones for these examples.

</details>

### [65] [Simple Self-Conditioning Adaptation for Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26985)
*Michael Cardei,Huu Binh Ta,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自条件化掩码扩散模型（SCMDM），通过对标准掩码扩散模型进行后训练适配，使每个去噪步骤能利用模型自身之前的干净状态预测，无需额外模型训练或推理开销，在多个领域显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散模型中，仍处于掩码状态的token会丢弃之前步骤的干净预测，必须仅从掩码token重新推断，这限制了跨步骤的精细化改进能力。现有部分自条件化方法需要从头训练模型，成本高昂。

Method: 提出SCMDM后训练适配方法：在每次去噪步骤中，将模型自身上一轮对干净状态的预测作为条件输入，仅需最小架构改动（如调整输入通道数），不引入循环潜状态路径，不依赖辅助参考模型，且采样时不增加额外去噪器评估次数。与需要从头训练的部分自条件化方法不同，SCMDM适用于任何预训练MDM。

Result: 在多个领域验证：OWT训练模型生成困惑度从42.89降至23.72（降低约50%）；离散图像合成质量、小分子生成、基因组分布建模等方面均显著优于标准MDM基线。

Conclusion: SCMDM是一种简单有效的后训练适配方法，大幅提升掩码扩散模型生成质量。研究表明在后训练场景下，部分自条件化中的50% dropout策略是次优的，当模型自生成干净状态估计变得有信息量后，专注于细化比混合条件与非条件目标更优。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate discrete sequences by iterative denoising under an absorbing masking process. In standard masked diffusion, if a token remains masked after a reverse update, the model discards its clean-state prediction for that position. Thus, still-masked positions must be repeatedly inferred from the mask token alone. This design choice limits cross-step refinement. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a simple, yet effective, post-training adaptation for MDMs that conditions each denoising step on the model's own previous clean-state predictions. The resulting method, called Self-Conditioned Masked Diffusion Models (SCMDM), requires minimal architectural change, does not introduce a recurrent latent-state pathway, does not rely on an auxiliary reference model, and adds no extra denoiser evaluations during sampling. This is an important departure from partial self-conditioning approaches which requires expensive model training from scratch. In particular, the paper shows that partial self-conditioning, including the commonly used 50% dropout strategy for training self-conditioned models from scratch, is suboptimal in the post-training regime. Instead, once the model's self-generated clean-state estimates become informative, the specialization to refinement is preferable to mixing conditional and unconditional objectives. SCMDM is evaluated across multiple domains, demonstrating consistent improvement over vanilla MDM baselines, achieving nearly a 50% reduction in generative perplexity on OWT-trained models (42.89 to 23.72), alongside strong improvements in discretized image synthesis quality, small molecular generation, and enhanced fidelity in genomic distribution modeling.

</details>


### [66] [People-Centred Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26991)
*Zheng Zhang,Milad Masroor,Cuong Nguyen,Tahir Hassan,Yuanhong Chen,David Rosewarne,Kevin Wells,Thanh-Toan Do,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PecMan框架，通过动态门控机制在医生工作负载约束下联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效率，并引入FairHAI基准来评估准确性、公平性和医生工作量之间的权衡。实验证明PecMan优于现有方法，为更可信和临床可行的AI系统铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心的医疗AI虽然取得了高准确率，但未能广泛临床采用。作者推测原因是缺乏对跨人群公平性能的优化以及工作流集成不足——性能偏见造成监管障碍，集成不良的自动化会干扰临床流程、降低人机协作质量。先前的工作流集成（如L2D、L2C）和AI公平性研究通常孤立地处理这些挑战，忽视了它们的相互依赖性和临床环境的实际约束（如医生可用性受限）。

Method: 提出People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan)框架，通过动态门控机制在医生工作负载约束下将病例分配给AI、医生或两者共同处理，联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流有效性。同时引入Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI)基准来评估准确性、公平性和医生工作量之间的权衡。

Result: 在FairHAI基准上的实验表明，PecMan在准确性、公平性和医生工作量的权衡上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PecMan通过联合优化公平性、准确性和工作流集成，为更可信和临床可行的AI系统铺平道路，有望推动医疗AI的临床实际采纳。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-centric medical AI have produced highly accurate diagnostic systems, but the emphasis on data curation and performance metrics has not translated into widespread clinical adoption. We conjecture that this limited uptake stems from insufficient attention dedicated to the optimisation of fair performance across diverse patient populations and to workflow integration: performance biases can create regulatory barriers, and poorly integrated automation can disrupt clinical routines, degrade the quality of human-AI collaboration, and reduce clinicians' willingness to adopt AI tools. Prior work on workflow integration (e.g., Learning to Defer (L2D) and Learning to Complement (L2C)) and AI fairness has typically examined these challenges in isolation, overlooking their natural interdependence and the practical constraints of clinical environments, such as restricted clinician availability. We propose People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan), a human-AI framework that jointly optimises fairness, diagnostic accuracy, and workflow effectiveness through a dynamic gating mechanism that assigns cases to AI, clinicians, or both under clinician workload constraints. We also introduce the Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI) benchmark for evaluating trade-offs between accuracy, fairness, and clinician workload. Experiments using this benchmark show that PecMan consistently outperforms existing methods, paving the way for more trustworthy and clinically viable AI systems. Code will be available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [67] [Automatic Causal Fairness Analysis with LLM-Generated Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27011)
*Alessia Berarducci,Eric Rossetto,Alessandro Antonucci,Marco Zaffalon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍FairMind原型工具，利用标准公平模型和反事实因果效应，在数据集层面自动化公平性分析，并通过LLM零样本生成报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML框架未充分考虑训练数据及预测结果中可能存在的公平性缺失问题，需要一种自动化方法在数据集层面进行公平性分析。

Method: 基于Plečko和Bareinboim提出的标准公平模型，通过反事实查询计算因果效应，实现对保护属性、混杂因子、中介变量及目标的公平性评估；采用封闭形式计算，并利用LLM（零样本）生成公平性分析报告。

Result: 通过示例展示了相比直接由LLM进行分析，该工具在公平性检测方面具有预期优势；还扩展到有序保护变量和连续目标，并提出了新的分解结果。

Conclusion: FairMind能够以因果推断为基础，自动化实现数据集层面的公平性审计，结合LLM报告生成，为AutoML系统引入公平性保障提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AutoML, intended as the process of automating the application of machine learning to real-world problems, is a key step for AI popularisation. Most AutoML frameworks are not accounting for the potential lack of fairness in the training data and in the corresponding predictions. We introduce \textsc{FairMind}, a software prototype aiming to automatise fairness analysis at the dataset level. We achieve that by resorting to the assumptions of the \emph{standard fairness model}, recently proposed by Plečko and Bareinboim. This allows for a sound fairness evaluation in terms of causal effects, based on \emph{counterfactual} queries involving the target, possibly confounders and mediators, and the different values of an input feature we regard as \emph{protected}. After the necessary data preprocessing, the tool implements a closed-form computation of the effects. LLMs are consequently exploited to generate accurate reports on the fairness levels detected in the training dataset. We achieve that in a zero-shot setup and show by examples the expected advantages with respect to a direct analysis performed by the LLM. To favour applications, extensions to ordinal protected variable and continuous targets and novel decomposition results are also discussed.

</details>


### [68] [Fidelity, Diversity, and Privacy: A Multi-Dimensional LLM Evaluation for Clinical Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27014)
*Guillermo Iglesias,Gema Bello-Orgaz,María Navas-Loro,Cristian Ramirez-Atencia,Mercè Salvador Robert,Enrique Baca-Garcia*

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5等大语言模型，基于ICD-10编码生成合成心理健康评估报告，并通过语义保真度、词汇多样性和隐私/抄袭三个维度评估生成文本，结果表明该方法能生成临床连贯、多样且隐私安全的合成数据，有效缓解心理健康领域标注数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康领域高质量标注医疗数据极度稀缺，且隐私法规限制了数据共享，导致鲁棒机器学习模型的训练面临瓶颈。

Method: 使用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5三种LLM，基于ICD-10编码条件生成合成心理健康评估报告；引入综合评估框架，从语义保真度、词汇多样性、隐私/抄袭三个维度对生成文本进行评估。

Result: 所有模型都能生成临床连贯、多样且隐私安全的合成报告，有效扩展了临床NLP任务的可用训练数据。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的合成数据生成是缓解心理健康领域标注数据稀缺问题的可行方案，能在不损害患者机密性的前提下扩充训练数据。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated medical data, particularly in mental health, poses a significant bottleneck for training robust machine learning models. Privacy regulations restrict data sharing, making synthetic data generation a promising alternative. The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in a data augmentation pipeline could be leveraged as an alternative in this field. In the proposed methodology, DeepSeek-R1, OpenBioLLM-Llama3 and Qwen 3.5 are used to generate synthetic mental health evaluation reports conditioned on specific International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision (ICD-10) codes. Because naive text generation can lead to mode collapse or privacy breaches (memorization), a comprehensive evaluation framework is introduced. The generated diagnostic texts are assessed across three dimensions: semantic fidelity, lexical diversity, and privacy/plagiarism. The results demonstrate that all models can generate clinically coherent, diverse, and privacy-safe synthetic reports, significantly expanding the available training data for clinical natural language processing tasks without compromising patient confidentiality.

</details>


### [69] [Dynamic Adversarial Fine-Tuning Reorganizes Refusal Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27019)
*Wenhao Lan,Shan Li,Junbin Yang,Haihua Shen,Yijun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过测量驱动的方法，研究7B语言模型在监督微调（SFT）和R2D2动态对抗微调下，安全对齐中拒绝行为的几何结构和因果机制变化。发现R2D2并非简单漂移，而是重组了拒绝载体的层位置，早期保持晚期层载体，后期迁移到早期层载体，有效秩保持低位（~1.23-1.27），表明低维但功能耦合的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽刻画了拒绝方向和越狱鲁棒性，但未解释动态对抗微调如何改变训练过程中拒绝载体的演化机制，特别是拒绝行为是简单漂移还是结构性重组尚不清楚。

Method: 在7B骨干模型上，对SFT和R2D2动态对抗微调进行对比实验。使用固定源HarmBench、StrongREJECT和XSTest作为评估基准，结合五锚点拒绝几何套件（测量拒绝载体的层位置、有效秩等）和因果干预分析。

Result: R2D2在步骤50和100时HarmBench ASR降至0.000，后回升至0.035（步骤250）和0.250（步骤500）；SFT的ASR维持在0.505-0.588。R2D2的XSTest any-refusal从1.000降至0.664和0.228。几何上，R2D2在步骤100前保持晚期层拒绝载体，之后迁移到早期层载体，有效秩稳定在1.23-1.27。因果干预显示低维但功能耦合的控制。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为的变化符合重组（reorganization）机制而非简单漂移：R2D2通过将拒绝载体从晚期层迁移到早期层来实现鲁棒性的动态调整，且拒绝控制是低维的但与模型效用存在耦合。结论受限于单一骨干模型和固定源攻击设置。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models must refuse harmful requests without collapsing into broad over-refusal, but the training-time mechanisms behind this tradeoff remain unclear. Prior work characterizes refusal directions and jailbreak robustness, yet does not explain how dynamic adversarial fine-tuning changes refusal carriers across training. We present a measurement-driven mechanism study, not a new defense, on one 7B backbone under supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and R2D2-style dynamic adversarial fine-tuning. Our protocol aligns fixed-source HarmBench, StrongREJECT, and XSTest with a five-anchor refusal-geometry suite and causal interventions. R2D2 drives fixed-source HarmBench ASR to 0.000 at steps 50 and 100, then partially reopens to 0.035 at step 250 and 0.250 at step 500; SFT remains less robust, with ASR between 0.505 and 0.588 at the same anchors. On XSTest, R2D2 any-refusal is 1.000 early, then falls to 0.664 and 0.228. Geometrically, R2D2 preserves a late-layer admissible carrier through step 100 before relocating to an early-layer carrier, while effective rank remains near 1.23--1.27. Causal interventions indicate low-dimensional but utility-coupled control. These results support a reorganization account rather than a drift-only account, with evidence limited to one backbone and fixed-source attacks.

</details>


### [70] [Cross-Subject Generalization for EEG Decoding: A Survey of Deep Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27033)
*Taida Li,Yujun Yan,Fei Dou,Wenzhan Song,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了深度学习在跨被试脑电解码中应对被试间差异（领域漂移）的方法，将其形式化为多源域问题，系统分类为特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四大类，并讨论了理论局限、被试身份价值及脑电基础模型等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑电解码中，不同被试间的个体差异导致严重领域漂移，使得在训练集上训练的模型难以泛化到未见过的测试被试，这一问题严重阻碍了脑电解码的实用化。

Method: 将跨被试设定形式化为多源域问题，提出严格的被试无关评估协议，并对现有文献进行系统分类，包括特征对齐（feature alignment）、对抗学习（adversarial learning）、特征解耦（feature disentanglement）和对比学习（contrastive learning）四大方法论家族。

Result: 系统梳理了四大类方法的原理与特点，识别出当前方法的理论局限性、被试身份的结构性价值以及脑电基础模型的涌现趋势这三个推动鲁棒脑电解码的关键要素。

Conclusion: 跨被试脑电泛化仍是开放挑战，未来需要突破现有方法的理论限制，善用被试身份信息，并关注脑电基础模型这一新兴方向。

Abstract: Deep learning for cross-subject EEG decoding is hindered by high inter-subject variability, which introduces a severe domain shift between training and unseen test subjects. This survey presents a comprehensive review of deep learning methodologies specifically engineered to address this cross-subject generalization challenge. To ground this analysis, we formalize the cross-subject setting as a multi-source domain problem and delineate the rigorous, subject-independent evaluation protocols required for valid assessment. Central to this survey is a systematic taxonomy of the current literature into discrete methodological families, including feature alignment, adversarial learning, feature disentanglement, and contrastive learning. We conclude by examining three critical elements for advancing robust, real-world decoding: the theoretical limitations of current methodologies, the structural value of subject identity, and the emergence of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [71] [Detecting Clinical Discrepancies in Health Coaching Agents: A Dual-Stream Memory and Reconciliation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27045)
*Samuel L Pugh,Eric Yang,Alexander Muir Sutherland,Alessandra Breschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双流记忆架构（Dual-Stream Memory Architecture），将患者叙述与结构化临床记录（FHIR）严格分离，并通过协调引擎（Reconciliation Engine）对记忆进行冲突检测与分类。在26名患者、675次纵向健康辅导会话的混合数据集上，该引擎检测出84.4%的临床差异，安全关键召回率达86.7%，并量化了13.6%的错误级联效应，证明验证患者自述记忆与临床记录的一致性对纵向健康智能体的安全部署至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体的记忆系统在纵向健康管理场景中面临双重挑战：患者自我报告具有时效性但易受回忆偏差影响，电子健康记录（EHR）经医学验证但常滞后。通用智能体记忆系统为追求连贯性，倾向于用最新用户陈述覆盖旧事实，这在临床数据应用中存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出双流记忆架构，包含（1）患者叙述流与结构化FHIR临床记录流的严格分离；（2）协调引擎，对每条提取记忆对照患者FHIR档案进行评估，按类型、严重程度及涉及的FHIR资源对差异进行分类。使用混合数据集（真实医患对话+基于FHIR的合成临床场景）在26名患者、675次会话上进行评估。

Result: 协调引擎检测到84.4%的设计临床差异，安全关键召回率为86.7%。通过将提取与协调评估耦合，量化出13.6%的错误级联效应，并追溯至从非结构化对话中提取记忆时丢失的临床细节，而非下游分类错误。

Conclusion: 验证了在纵向健康场景中，将患者自述记忆与临床记录进行交叉验证既可行又必要，这是确保纵向健康智能体安全部署的关键环节。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents transition from single-session tools to persistent systems managing longitudinal healthcare journeys, their memory architectures face a critical challenge: reconciling two imperfect sources of truth. The patient's evolving self-report is current but prone to recall bias, while the Electronic Health Record (EHR) is medically validated but frequently stale. General-purpose agent memory systems optimize for coherence by overwriting older facts with the user's latest statement, a pattern that risks safety failures when applied to clinical data. We introduce a Dual-Stream Memory Architecture that strictly separates the patient narrative from the structured clinical record (FHIR), governed by a dedicated Reconciliation Engine that evaluates every extracted memory against the patient's FHIR profile and classifies discrepancies by type, severity, and the specific FHIR resources involved. We evaluate this architecture on 26 patients across 675 longitudinal wellness coaching sessions, using a hybrid dataset that interleaves real provider-patient transcripts with synthetic, FHIR-grounded clinical scenarios. In isolated testing, the engine detects 84.4% of designed clinical discrepancies with 86.7% safety-critical recall. By coupling extraction and reconciliation evaluation on the same data, we directly quantify a 13.6% error cascade, tracing the degradation to clinical details lost during memory extraction from unstructured conversation rather than to downstream classification errors. These findings establish that validating patient-reported memories against clinical records is both feasible and necessary for safe deployment of longitudinal health agents.

</details>


### [72] [Learning Rate Transfer in Normalized Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27077)
*Boris Shigida,Boris Hanin,Andrey Gromov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出νGPT，通过结合对齐指数修正μP方法，解决了nGPT在模型宽度、深度和token序列长度上无法进行学习率迁移的问题，实现了跨维度超参数迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nGPT虽能加速训练且无需权重衰减或学习率预热，但其超参数无法在模型维度和token序列长度间迁移，限制了其可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 结合数值实验与对齐指数（arXiv:2407.05872），重新审视并修改μP超参数迁移方法（arXiv:2011.14522），设计新型nGPT参数化方案νGPT。

Result: νGPT在宽度（width）、深度（depth）和token序列长度（token horizon）上均实现了学习率的有效迁移。

Conclusion: νGPT通过原则性的参数化修正，成功实现了跨模型维度的学习率迁移，解决了nGPT的关键局限性。

Abstract: The Normalized Transformer, or nGPT (arXiv:2410.01131) achieves impressive training speedups and does not require weight decay or learning rate warmup. However, despite having hyperparameters that explicitly scale with model size, we observe that nGPT does not exhibit learning rate transfer across model dimension and token horizon. To rectify this, we combine numerical experiments with a principled use of alignment exponents (arXiv:2407.05872) to revisit and modify the $μ$P approach to hyperparameter transfer (arXiv:2011.14522). The result is a novel nGPT parameterization we call $ν$GPT. Through extensive empirical validation, we find $ν$GPT exhibits learning rate transfer across width, depth, and token horizon.

</details>


### [73] [Co-Evolving Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27083)
*Naibin Gu,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Chuanyu Qin,Dingyu Yao,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD)方法，通过并行训练专家并在RLVR训练过程中引入在线策略蒸馏（双向教师），解决混合RLVR的能力冲突和流水线OPD的知识吸收不足问题，实现文本、图像、视频推理能力的全能整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练范式（混合RLVR和流水线OPD）在整合多专家能力时分别存在能力冲突和行为模式差距导致的知识吸收不足问题，亟需一种能兼顾专家一致性行为模式和互补知识的整合方法。

Method: CoPD采用并行专家训练范式，在每个专家的RLVR训练过程中（而非完成后）引入在线策略蒸馏（OPD），并使专家之间互为教师进行双向蒸馏，实现共同进化。

Result: 实验表明CoPD在文本、图像、视频推理能力的全合一整合上显著优于混合RLVR和MOPD等强基线，甚至超越领域特化专家模型。

Conclusion: CoPD提出的模型并行训练范式可能启发一种新的训练扩展范式，有效解决了多能力整合中的行为模式不一致和知识互补性不足问题。

Abstract: RLVR and OPD have become standard paradigms for post-training. We provide a unified analysis of these two paradigms in consolidating multiple expert capabilities into a single model, identifying capability loss in different ways: mixed RLVR suffers from inter-capability divergence cost, while the pipeline of first training experts and then performing OPD, though avoiding divergence, fails to fully absorb teacher capabilities due to large behavioral pattern gaps between teacher and student. We propose Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD), which encourages parallel training of experts and introduces OPD during each expert's ongoing RLVR training rather than after complete expert training, with experts serving as mutual teachers (making OPD bidirectional) to co-evolve. This enables more consistent behavioral patterns among experts while maintaining sufficient complementary knowledge throughout. Experiments validate that CoPD achieves all-in-one integration of text, image, and video reasoning capabilities, significantly outperforming strong baselines such as mixed RLVR and MOPD, and even surpassing domain-specific experts. The model parallel training pattern offered by CoPD may inspire a novel training scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [74] [AutoSP: Unlocking Long-Context LLM Training Via Compiler-Based Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27089)
*Ahan Gupta,Zhihao Wang,Neel Dani,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSP是首个自动优化LLM长上下文训练的解决方案，通过自动序列并行和长上下文感知的激活检查点技术，在NVIDIA和AMD硬件上分别将训练上下文长度提升2.7倍和2.5倍，且对运行时性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练库专注于大参数模型的优化（如ZeRO-3/FSDP、张量/流水线并行），缺乏对长上下文训练的高层抽象，用户需要重写训练库以集成序列并行等复杂优化，开发效率低且需要深度专业知识。

Method: AutoSP通过编译模型自动应用一组针对性优化：自动化序列并行（auto sequence parallelism）和长上下文感知的激活检查点（long-context aware activation-checkpointing），无需用户手动干预。

Result: 在NVIDIA硬件上训练上下文长度提升至2.7倍，在AMD硬件上提升至2.5倍，且对运行时性能影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: AutoSP作为首个自动化长上下文训练优化方案，有效降低了长上下文训练的实现门槛，同时跨不同硬件平台实现了显著的上下文长度扩展和可忽略的性能开销。

Abstract: Large-language-models (LLMs) demonstrate enormous utility in long-context tasks which require processing prompts that consist of tens to hundreds of thousands of tokens. However, existing LLM training libraries do not provide easy to use abstractions to optimize for long-context training, instead focusing on optimizations for models with large parameter counts through ZeRO-3/FSDP, Tensor and Pipeline parallelism. This forces users to rewrite LLM training libraries to incorporate compositions of various complex long-context optimizations, such as sequence-parallelism, to training pipelines; a process that requires in-depth expertise, reducing developer productivity. To tackle these challenges, we introduce AutoSP: the first automated solution to automatically optimize LLM training for longer-contexts. AutoSP compiles models and applies a targeted set of optimizations: automated sequence parallelism, and long-context aware activation-checkpointing, to drastically enhance LLM trainability at negligible cost to throughput. Our evaluation demonstrates AutoSP's capability on both NVIDIA and AMD hardware, increasing training contexts by upto 2.7$\times$ and 2.5$\times$ respectively over competitive hand-written baseline at negligible cost to runtime performance.

</details>


### [75] [Anomaly Detection in Soil Heavy Metal Contamination Using Unsupervised Learning for Environmental Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27102)
*Isaac Tettey Adjokatse,Samuel Senyo Koranteng,George Yamoah Afrifa,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Marcellin Atemkeng,Joseph Bremang Tandoh,Kow Ahor Essel-Yorke,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究采用无监督机器学习框架（Isolation Forest、PCA重建误差、DBSCAN及共识方法）识别加纳中央区域12个废物堆放点土壤中重金属异常污染模式。共识方法识别出6个稳健异常样本（7.7%），均集中于S3站点，其Hazard Index均值高出正常样本70–80%。PCA重建误差与HI强正相关（r≈0.8），揭示了三种异常类型：S3极端Cu富集、S4/S5异常低Ni、S9–S12中等多金属（Pb–Zn）共升高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加纳快速城市化区域中，未受监管的废物堆放点导致重金属土壤污染持续威胁环境与公众健康，传统健康风险指数（如HI、ILCR）虽能评估风险但缺乏对异常模式的细粒度识别能力，亟需客观、数据驱动的异常检测方法来辅助精准环境管理。

Method: 采集加纳中央区域12个废物站点及居民对照区共78个土壤样本，分析8种重金属（As、Cd、Cr、Cu、Hg、Ni、Pb、Zn）浓度并计算HI和ILCR指标。采用四种无监督学习方法：Isolation Forest、PCA重建误差（各识别12个异常）、DBSCAN（未检测到离群点）及共识方法（取三者交集，识别6个稳健异常）。通过PCA重建误差与HI的相关性分析验证多变量偏离与健康风险的一致性。

Result: Isolation Forest与PCA重建误差各识别12个异常样本（15.4%），DBSCAN未发现密度隔离噪声点。共识方法识别6个稳健异常（7.7%），全部集中在S3站点。异常样本平均HI值比正常样本高70–80%，且所有共识异常均超过HI=1阈值。PCA重建误差与HI呈强正相关（r≈0.8）。三种异常类型：S3极端Cu富集、S4/S5异常低Ni、S9–S12中等多金属（Pb–Zn）共升高。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习能够超越传统聚合指数，提供更细粒度、客观的异常污染模式洞见，有助于实现有针对性的场地优先排序和基于风险的环境管理决策。

Abstract: Soil contamination by heavy metals poses a persistent environmental and public health concern in rapidly urbanising regions of Ghana, particularly at unregulated waste disposal sites. This study applies an unsupervised machine learning framework to detect and characterise anomalous heavy metal contamination patterns in soils from twelve waste sites and residential controls in the Central Region, of Ghana. Concentrations of eight metals (As, Cd, Cr, Cu, Hg, Ni, Pb, Zn) were analysed alongside standard health risk indices, including the Hazard Index (HI) and Incremental Lifetime Cancer Risk (ILCR). Isolation Forest and PCA reconstruction error each identified $12$ anomalous samples ($15.4\%$ of $78$ samples), while DBSCAN detected no density-isolated noise points. A consensus approach isolated six robust anomalies ($7.7\%)$, all spatially concentrated at a single site (S3). Anomalies exhibited approximately $70$--$80\%$ higher mean HI values than normal samples, with all consensus anomalies exceeding the HI$=1$ threshold. PCA reconstruction error showed a strong positive association with HI ($r \approx 0.8$), indicating consistency between multivariate deviation and health risk. Three distinct anomaly types were identified: extreme Cu enrichment at S3, anomalously low Ni at S4/S5, and moderate multi-metal (Pb--Zn) co-elevation at S9--S12. The results demonstrate that unsupervised machine learning provides granular, objective insight beyond aggregate indices, enabling targeted site prioritisation and risk-informed environmental management.

</details>


### [76] [Better Models, Faster Training: Sigmoid Attention for single-cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27124)
*Vijay Sadashivaiah,Georgios Dasoulas,Judith Mueller,Soumya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用sigmoid注意力机制替代softmax注意力机制来训练稳定的生物基础模型，在六个单细胞数据集上实现了更强的细胞类型分离、更快的训练速度和更稳定的训练过程，并开源了高效GPU内核TritonSigmoid。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练稳定的生物基础模型需要重新思考注意力机制，因为softmax注意力存在固有的不稳定性，导致训练过程中梯度爆炸等问题。

Method: 使用sigmoid注意力作为softmax注意力的直接替代品（drop-in replacement），并分析了sigmoid注意力的全局有界导数（≤0.25）和对角Jacobian结构，这些特性有助于缓解训练不稳定性。同时实现了高效的TritonSigmoid GPU内核。

Result: 在六个单细胞数据集上，sigmoid注意力相比softmax：细胞类型分离提升25%，训练速度提升10%，训练更稳定。在1.6亿参数的双向注意力模型压力测试中，softmax梯度爆炸四个数量级而sigmoid保持稳定。TritonSigmoid在H100 GPU上达到515 TFLOPS，超过FlashAttention-2和FlashSigmoid。

Conclusion: sigmoid注意力在理论和经验上均优于softmax注意力，适合用于生物基础模型的训练。

Abstract: Training stable biological foundation models requires rethinking attention mechanisms: we find that using sigmoid attention as a drop in replacement for softmax attention a) produces better learned representations: on six diverse single-cell datasets, sigmoid achieves 25% higher cell-type separation, better cell-type cohesion metrics, and lower validation loss, b) faster training, models with sigmoid attention train up to 10% faster than their softmax counterparts, and c) more stable training by eliminating inherent sources of instability in softmax attention. We establish that sigmoid attention has globally bounded derivatives ($\leq 0.25$) as opposed to softmax, and a diagonal Jacobian structure in contrast with softmax's dense coupling, which together help alleviate training instabilities. In stress tests on 160M-parameter bidirectional attention models trained without gradient clipping on 8K-token sequences, softmax diverges catastrophically, with gradients exploding by four orders of magnitude, while sigmoid remains stable. Finally, we implement and open-source TritonSigmoid, an efficient GPU kernel that achieves 515 TFLOPS on H100 GPUs, outperforming both FlashAttention-2 and FlashSigmoid, with native padding support, which is essential for biological sequences. Our results establish sigmoid attention as both theoretically grounded and empirically superior for biological foundation models. Code is available at https://github.com/MSDLLCpapers/triton-sigmoid

</details>


### [77] [How to Guide Your Flow: Few-Step Alignment via Flow Map Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27147)
*Jerry Y. Huang,Justin Lin,Sheel Shah,Kartik Nair,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流图奖励引导（FMRG），将引导问题重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，利用流图实现无需训练、单轨迹的高效生成引导，在文本到图像任务中以仅3次NFE达到或超越基线，实现了至少一个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导方法要么需要昂贵的多粒子、多步方案，要么依赖理解不充分的近似，缺乏高效且理论清晰的方法。

Method: 将引导重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，推导出包含现有方法的分层算法体系；基于流图在最优解中的自然出现，提出FMRG框架，利用流图同时实现积分与引导。

Result: 在文本到图像尺度上，FMRG在逆问题、风格迁移、人类偏好和VLM奖励等任务中仅用3次NFE即可匹配或超越基线，相比之前最先进方法实现至少一个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: FMRG是一种无需训练、单轨迹的引导方法，通过最优控制理论和流图实现了高效、高质量的生成引导，显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: In generative modeling, we often wish to produce samples that maximize a user-specified reward such as aesthetic quality or alignment with human preferences, a problem known as guidance. Despite their widespread use, existing guidance methods either require expensive multi-particle, many-step schemes or rely on poorly understood approximations. We reformulate guidance as a deterministic optimal control problem, yielding a hierarchy of algorithms that subsumes existing approaches at the coarsest level. We show that the flow map, an object of significant recent interest for its role in fast inference, arises naturally in the optimal solution. Based on this observation, we propose Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG): a training-free, single-trajectory framework that uses the flow map to both integrate and guide the flow. At text-to-image scale, FMRG matches or surpasses baselines across inverse problems, style transfer, human preferences, and VLM rewards with as few as 3 NFEs, giving at least an order-of-magnitude speedup in comparison to prior state of the art.

</details>


### [78] [Generalizing the Geometry of Model Merging Through Frechet Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27155)
*Marvin F. da Silva,Mohammed Adnan,Felix Dangel,Sageev Oore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将模型合并视为Fréchet平均，即在合适的流形上最小化测地距离之和，并强调合并过程本身必须具有对称性不变性；针对LoRA的对称性，提出了商流形上的实用合并算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（如参数空间平均）未考虑架构对称性，导致合并结果不稳定；需要一种从几何根本上具有对称性不变性的合并框架。

Method: 将模型合并形式化为Fréchet平均（流形上最小化测地距离之和），指出关键设计在于度量、流形和距离近似的选择；在简化假设下证明Fréchet平均包含Fisher合并；针对LoRA的对称性，将其建模为商流形上的合并问题，提出实用算法。

Result: 从理论上统一了模型合并的几何框架，证明Fréchet平均可涵盖Fisher合并；分析了现有LoRA合并方法的局限性，提出的商流形算法在对称性感知上优于朴素方法。

Conclusion: 对称性感知的合并必须同时考虑几何和平均过程的对称性不变性；LoRA的对称性结构天然适合商流形建模，该框架为更广泛的模型合并问题提供了通用视角。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple models into one without additional training. Naïve parameter-space averaging can be fragile under architectural symmetries, as their geometry does not take them into account. In this work we show that not only the geometry, but also the averaging procedure itself, must be symmetry-invariant to achieve symmetry-aware merges. Consequently, we propose a general solution: merging as Fréchet averaging, i.e., selecting parameters that minimize a sum of geodesic distances on an appropriate manifold. In this view, the key design choice is the overall geometry, i.e., the choice of metric, manifold, and distance approximation, that determines what it means for two models to be "close". We show that Fréchet averaging, combined with simplifying assumptions, contains Fisher merging. Building on this, we examine the particular case of low-rank adapters (LoRA), whose symmetries induce a distinct geometry: that of a quotient manifold. We outline the limitations of current LoRA merging methods, propose a practical algorithm for this setting, and show how they compare with other commonly used approaches.

</details>


### [79] [Distributional Alignment Games for Answer-Level Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27166)
*Mehryar Mohri,Jon Schneider,Yifan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于博弈论的分布对齐框架，将答案级微调（ALFT）中难以处理的边际化问题转化为可解的投影问题，通过策略与目标分布的双人博弈达到纳什均衡，统一了多样性与自改进方法，并在数学推理任务中通过Coherence-GRPO算法取得显著复杂度增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接优化答案级目标需要在大规模潜在推理路径空间上进行边际化，计算上不可行，因此需要一种可计算的方法来高效地实现答案级微调。

Method: 将ALFT提升为分布对齐博弈，构建策略（生成器）与目标（辅助分布）之间的双人博弈，证明纳什均衡等价于原答案级优化问题的解，从而将不可解的边际化问题转化为可解的投影问题，并设计兼容GRPO的高效算法（如Coherence-GRPO）。

Result: 该框架统一了多样性与自改进（一致性）方法，在数学推理任务上通过Coherence-GRPO实现了显著的复杂度增益。

Conclusion: 博弈论视角下的分布对齐框架为答案级微调提供了一种高效且统一的解决方案，将原本计算不可行的问题转化为可处理的形式。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of \emph{Answer-Level Fine-Tuning} (ALFT), where the goal is to optimize a language model based on the correctness or properties of its final answers, rather than the specific reasoning traces used to produce them. Directly optimizing answer-level objectives is computationally intractable due to the need to marginalize over the vast space of latent reasoning paths. To overcome this, we propose a general game-theoretical framework that lifts the problem to a \emph{Distributional Alignment Game}. We formulate ALFT as a two-player game between a Policy (the generator) and a Target (an auxiliary distribution). We prove that the Nash Equilibrium of this game corresponds exactly to the solution of the original answer-level optimization problem. This variational perspective transforms the intractable marginalization problem into a tractable projection problem. We demonstrate that this framework unifies recent approaches to diversity and self-improvement (coherence) and provide efficient algorithms compatible with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), such as Coherence-GRPO, yielding significant complexity gains in mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Global Optimality for Constrained Exploration via Penalty Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28144)
*Florian Wolf,Ilyas Fatkhullin,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出策略梯度惩罚（PGP）方法，一种单循环策略空间方法，通过二次惩罚正则化处理受限最大熵探索问题，在非凸策略参数化下实现了全局最后迭代收敛保证，达到ε-最优约束熵值且约束违反被ε界限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的强化学习探索常受安全、资源或模仿等约束，而最大熵探索在约束设置下缺乏可加性结构，贝尔曼方程方法不适用；现有方法（Ying et al., 2025）仅提供弱遗憾和遍历平均保证，无法保证最终输出策略的近似最优性和可行性。

Method: 提出PGP方法，利用二次惩罚正则化处理一般凸占用度量约束；通过构造伪奖励获得惩罚目标的梯度估计，利用经典策略梯度定理；建立惩罚目标的正则性和光滑性性质；利用隐藏凸性和强对偶性证明收敛性。

Result: 建立了全局最后迭代收敛保证，达到ε-最优约束熵值且约束违反被ε界限制；在网格世界基准上进行消融实验验证；在两个具有挑战性的连续控制任务上展示可扩展性。

Conclusion: PGP方法有效解决了受限最大熵探索问题，在非凸策略参数化下实现了强于现有方法的收敛保证（最后迭代而非遍历平均），且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Efficient exploration is a central problem in reinforcement learning and is often formalized as maximizing the entropy of the state-action occupancy measure. While unconstrained maximum-entropy exploration is relatively well understood, real-world exploration is often constrained by safety, resource, or imitation requirements. This constrained setting is particularly challenging because entropy maximization lacks additive structure, rendering Bellman-equation-based methods inapplicable.
